# Graph Report - Reasoner  (2026-06-09)

## Corpus Check
- 2737 files · ~2,684,961 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 37434 nodes · 54052 edges · 3088 communities (2595 shown, 493 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 79% EXTRACTED · 21% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 11561 edges (avg confidence: 0.57)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `06de96d7`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
